Ward reports

A report for every one of the 7943 electoral wards in Great Britain, grouped by the local authority they sit in. Wards are often smaller than a neighbourhood (LSOA), so each neighbourhood is divided between the wards it covers according to where its residents live. That split is a well-founded estimate rather than a measurement, so read figures for small wards with more caution.
